### Decisions taken in B3 (D-77)

None of these opens a question. Each is the most reasonable default within the BUILD_SPEC author's remit.

| Id | Decision | Rationale |
|---|---|---|
| BS4-D-01 | SF-04 (RT-25 `/schedules`) and SF-06:entries (RT-30 `/journals/entries`) are built by RPS-7, directly after the report framework, although PHASES §10.1 assigns their route rows to CLO. CLO-9 builds the date-range journal view (REQ-JE-008) and CLO-20 the schedule release that SF-04 displays; both cite RT-25 and RT-30. GATE-CLO counts these two rows as transferred to RPS-7 and GATE-RPS verifies their captures | SCREENS_B §3.5 and §4.1 bind `POST /report-runs` with `legacy_je_summary` and `revenue_waterfall`, and T-RPT-01, T-RPT-02 and API-R-41 are created in RPS (PHASES §5.1, §5.2). Building the screens in CLO would depend on a later item (header XR-19) or ship a placeholder (XR-14) |
| BS4-D-02 | CLO-1 adds `tg_journal_line__period_guard` to `journal_line`, the DB-07 guard of `subledger_line` applied to journal lines with the same error code `EREV-LED-003` | REQ-CLS-002 and J-13-AC-5 require that any subledger or journal insert into a `closed` or `permanently_locked` period is refused by a database trigger; 04 DB-07 names only `subledger_line` |
| BS4-D-03 | A close run executes its 14 steps once, in T-CLS-01 order, in one `CLOSE_RUN` job. `EXPORT`, `ACKNOWLEDGEMENT_WAIT` and `GL_TIE_OUT` are observation steps: when reached they record the current state ("<n> batches exported", "<n> of <m> batches acknowledged", the SUBLEDGER_TO_GL difference, or "No trial balance attached") and end `SUCCEEDED`; they never export, acknowledge or reconcile. The `LOCK` step stays `PENDING` when the run finishes; the lock job sets it `SUCCEEDED` with "Locked by <name>". The run status is `SUCCEEDED` when steps 1 to 13 succeeded. Rev 1.14 (supervisor ruling R-79 (b)): T-CLS-01's order is the display order of the `steps` array; the job executes `FX_REMEASUREMENT` before `RELEASE_SCHEDULES` (05 RCP-08 rev 1.9: the FX pass, then the release pass, then the netting reclass) and every other step in the order of the array; a resumed run restarts at the first step, in execution order, that is not `SUCCEEDED` | PRD J-13.7 requires a `SUCCEEDED` close run while the journal run is still `draft`; export, acknowledgement, reconciliation sign-off and lock are separate audited commands (J-13.8 to J-13.14) whose outcomes the lock gates enforce (CTL-016, CTL-021). The E-62 literal set has no skipped status |
| BS4-D-04 | Step `DATASET_FREEZE` writes the twelve E-64 datasets of the period as `SNAPSHOT_DATASET` files and records their hashes in `close_run.counts.datasets`. Rev 1.21 (lane F-CLO-B's pre-build note, accepted by the supervisor on 2026-09-30; ENGINE_SPEC_B S15-R-18 rev 1.82): the step freezes all twelve at ONE instant of its own — the later of its application instant and its transaction timestamp, the lock's rule — and not at the run's `cutoff_known_at`, which is the instant the run began and lies before the run's own postings (at that instant eleven datasets would leave them out while `JE_POPULATION` held the journal lines that summarise them). The lock decision freezes again at its own cutoff (S15-R-18c) and takes no dataset it did not compute there; the file store keeps one file per content, so a dataset whose bytes are unchanged at the lock is the file the run wrote and `lock_snapshot.file_id` names it, and one whose content moved — `JE_POPULATION` in an ordinary close, whose journal run is approved, exported and acknowledged in between — is a file of its own. The lock does not skip the computation of a dataset: that no group became dirty does not prove a dataset unchanged | T-CLS-05 requires `period_lock_id`, which does not exist before lock; PERF-25 and DG-PERF-02 measure the freeze inside the close run |
| BS4-D-05 | Cross-author prerequisites cite `GATE-<code>` of the phase that provides the interface, together with the dev-guide signature or 04 id, because the other authors' phase files are written concurrently and their item numbers are not yet fixed | PHASES §0 cross-author interfaces |
| BS4-D-06 | Tenant-defined close tasks (REQ-CLS-021) are maintained through `GET, POST /close-checklist-templates` and `PATCH /close-checklist-templates/{id}` (IM-M, `If-Match`; read `config.read`, write `settings.manage`), catalogued with API-R-18 in the OpenAPI document | 04 §15.3 has no route for T-CLS-02 rows, although T-CLS-02 holds tenant-defined tasks and REQ-CLS-021 is release 1.0 |
| BS4-D-07 | A checklist waiver (`POST /periods/{id}/checklist/{item_id}/waive`) creates an approval request of subject `EXCEPTION_WAIVER` whose `SUBJECTS` entry covers table `close_checklist_item`; routing is `exception.waive` held by a user other than the requester | E-08 has no checklist subject, and PRD §2.5 routes `EXCEPTION_WAIVER` to a user other than the requester and the owner |
| BS4-D-08 | A lock request with failing gates returns 409 `close-gates-failed` with one `errors[]` entry per failing gate (`rule_id` = the `gate_check_code`) and the ERR-14 detail | 04 §15.2 (rev 1.1) owns the slug (D-73); the rev 1.0 wording `invalid-transition` in 04 §16.8 predates it |
| BS4-D-09 | The auto-certification rule `AUTO-REC-01` is a rule of rule set kind `AUTO_APPROVAL`. CLO-17 extends `erev_engine.rules.FIELDS["AUTO_APPROVAL"]` additively with `reconciliation.kind`, `reconciliation.variance_count` and `reconciliation.unexplained_other_amount` | E-55 has no certification kind, and T-CLS-06 stores `auto_certify_rule_set_version_id` and `auto_certify_rule_id` |
| BS4-D-10 | `@pytest.mark.control` tests of CLO, RPS, SNP and AIX are named `test_ctl_<nnn>_<slug>` so that a grep for the control id finds them; other named tests follow DG-LAY-06 | DG-TST-06; BS-D-22 |
| BS4-D-11 | Creating a scenario runs a `TENANT_SNAPSHOT` job with purpose `SANDBOX_COPY`, whose completion writes the T-FC-01 row in the production tenant | SCREENS_B §7.1 binds `POST /scenarios` to job `TENANT_SNAPSHOT`, and T-PLT-34 has no scenario purpose |
| BS4-D-12 | The Anthropic adapter follows 05 AIA-07: it sends no server-side `fallbacks` parameter, although the `claude-api` skill proposes one by default for `claude-opus-5`; `stop_reason = "refusal"` marks the proposal `failed` with `AI_REFUSAL`. The adapter omits `thinking`, keeps only `text` content blocks and receives its SDK client through an injected factory, so that tests use a stub client | A fallback model would change the configuration evidence without a configuration change (AIA-07); Claude Opus 5 runs adaptive thinking when `thinking` is omitted, and header XR-11 requires tests against a mocked client |
| BS4-D-13 | Prompt template ids are `contract-review`, `explain-narrative`, `anomaly-summary` and `revenue-qa`, each at version 1 | PRD J-19.5 names only `contract-review` version 1; AIA-08 fixes the directory layout |
| BS4-D-14 | A capture whose SCREENS_B §15 state needs a journey that DMO builds uses seeded or spec-created state until that journey item moves it (for example a sandbox copy created by the spec for `sf-15-sandbox`, a spec-run proposal for `sf-20`, the seeded FY2026-P08 lock for `sf-05-locked`) | PHASES BS-D-09 |
| BS4-D-15 | Industry demo tenants are asserted on structure, mandatory scenarios and the equality between the emitted expected-output files and the seeded tenant's figures; each dataset README maps scenario ids to answer-key ids. Equality with answer-key values is not asserted | PRD §2.10 asserts only structure for these tenants and re-anchors scenario dates to 2026, which changes date-driven figures |
| BS4-D-16 | The G9 close measurement, under BS4-D-03, runs a first close run per entity, then the harness approves, exports (CSV) and acknowledges the journal runs and attaches the trial balance, then a second close run per entity whose observation steps record the full export, acknowledgement and tie-out; wall time runs from the first `POST /close-runs` to the last second-pass `DATASET_FREEZE` | DG-PERF-02 lists export, acknowledgement and tie-out inside the measured window, while BS4-D-03 makes those steps observations |
| BS4-D-17 | Deploy static tests live in `backend/tests/unit/deploy/` and document presence tests in `backend/tests/unit/docs/`; Terraform uses the flat file names of DG §1.1 | DG-TST-03 unit layer; 05 DPL-30 rev 1.1 lets the dev guide govern file names |
| BS4-D-18 | For WLD-T-01, REQ-DEMO-003 is met by the seeded Jun 2026 lock with reopen (WLD-P-03) and invalid import (WLD-B-04), plus J-05, J-06 and J-07, which create the prospective modification, the catch-up and the VC re-estimate in the e2e world | PRD §2.2, §2.9 and the serial journey design (BS-D-05) produce these objects in journeys, not in the seed |
| BS4-D-19 | The G1 requirement comparison reads "REQs completed" from PROGRESS.md and from the ticking commit bodies, and strips any "REQs contributed" text, so checkpoint collapsing (header §6) cannot hide requirement ids | Header §6 collapses ticked items to one line each |
| BS4-D-20 | SNP has 5 items and FCS 8 items against the indicative 6 and 10 of PHASES §1 | Within the permitted third; every membership id is still cited |

## 15 CLO Close, schedules, journals and reconciliations

**Entry criteria.** GATE-EDS ticked (PHASES §1 row 15).

**Exit criteria (PHASES §1 row CLO).** (1) CLO tables of PHASES §5.1 (T-SL-05 to T-SL-10, T-CLS-01 to T-CLS-08), API-R rows of §5.2 (API-R-18 `cockpit`, `checklist`, close, lock, reopen and permanent-lock commands, `locks`, `transitions`; API-R-37, API-R-38, API-R-39, API-R-40) and the provisioning extension of §5.3 (system `close_checklist_template` rows for every gate check code). (2) Period transitions SM-07 (DB-07): soft close, close gates, lock with certification, reopen with dual approval, permanent lock; close runs with the 14 steps of SCREENS_B §1.2 (05 RCP-19; PERF-01 steps); schedule release, FX remeasurement pass and netting reclass; lock snapshots (`erev_api/domain/close/snapshots.py`). (3) Journal summarisation and export (ENGINE_SPEC_B §14.3): runs, batches, grain, the CSV adapter and the NetSuite and QuickBooks Online mock adapters, outbox relay, acknowledgements; manual adjustments, manual release and defer. (4) Billing-to-subledger and subledger-to-GL reconciliations with the mock trial balance pull and auto-certification; multi-entity close. (5) CLO screens of §10; J-23. (6) Tagged tests of §7 for CLO.

**Membership.**

      - `backend/tests/domain/close/test_gates.py::test_the_approvals_gates_own_waiver_request_is_no_pending_approval_of_its_period` — rev 1.90 (item CLO-APPROVALS-WAIVER-OWN-REQUEST-1 — PRODUCT DEFECT, measured on 2026-10-03; 04 §16.8 `blockers` rev 1.318; PRD BR-CLS-01 rev 1.206), request by request for September 2026 in soft close and August: the waiver request of September's `APPROVALS_CLEARED` item is left out of September's `approvals_pending` and counted in August's; the waiver request of September's `EXCEPTIONS_CLEARED` item and that of an exception item of the entity are counted in both; the gate says September's count and sentence — where September counted the waiver request of its own approvals gate; the last step of the witness of rev 1.86 above states the same; and the rule `::test_the_approvals_gates_own_waiver_request_is_named_by_type_subject_and_gate` of `test_own_lock_request_rules.py` — the predicate is the type, the subject and the item's gate together
      - `backend/tests/domain/close/test_gates.py::test_ctl_002_failed_interface_run_fails_gate` — tagged `@pytest.mark.control("CTL-002")`; a failed import upload with an open `CONTROL_TOTALS_MISMATCH` item makes `INTERFACES_COMPLETE` `FAILED` with count 1
      - Witnesses of the security review and the rulings of 2026-09-30 (header rev 1.37):
        - `backend/tests/domain/close/test_gates.py::test_ctl_002_failed_sync_runs_hold_the_gate_until_cleared` — a `sync_run` of a connection of the entity that ended `FAILED` or `CONTROL_TOTAL_MISMATCH` (E-72) counts in `INTERFACES_COMPLETE` and `blockers.interface_failures` until a later run of the same connection and kind succeeded over the same window or its failure items are all cleared; a `TEST_CONNECTION` probe never counts (04 §16.8 rev 1.106; supervisor rulings R-45 (d), R-56 (a), item CLO-GATE-SYNC-1 — header rev 1.37: the clearing rule replaces "within the period", which the gate never read)
        - `backend/tests/domain/close/test_gates.py::test_data_quality_warning_holds_no_lock` — a `WARNING` finding of the monitors counts neither in `EXCEPTIONS_CLEARED` nor in `blockers.exceptions_open`; a `BLOCKING` one counts in both gates; a `WARNING` item of another source still counts (04 Table 15.4-E rev 1.106; supervisor ruling R-57 — header rev 1.37)
        - `backend/tests/api/test_cockpit.py::test_sc_8_cockpit_reads_write_no_audit_event_and_only_a_changed_checklist` and `::test_sc_8_monitors_run_before_the_period_commands_and_never_on_a_read` — a cockpit or checklist GET appends no audit event, writes a checklist row only when it differs and runs no monitor; the monitors run as SYSTEM before `start-close` and `request-lock` (04 §16.8 rev 1.106; supervisor ruling R-32, security finding SC-8 — header rev 1.37)
        - `backend/tests/api/test_cockpit.py::test_never_waivable_gates_refuse_a_waiver` — `JE_BALANCED`, `JE_COMPLETE` and `CONTROLLER_CERTIFIED` carry `is_waivable = false` and their waive command is 409 (supervisor ruling R-55 (c), security finding SC-N4 — header rev 1.37); rev 1.24: and `CLOSE_RUN_COMPLETED` (supervisor ruling R-114 (b))
      - `backend/tests/domain/close/test_gates.py::test_ctl_049_unreviewed_judgement_fails_gate` — tagged `@pytest.mark.control("CTL-049")`; a `SUBMITTED` judgement record for a contract of the entity makes `JUDGEMENTS_REVIEWED` `FAILED` (count 1); after review by a user other than the author it is `PASSED`

        - `backend/tests/unit/close/test_close_run_gate_result.py` — the gate as a pure rule: the thirteenth of fourteen with the certification last; "Close run not completed" without a run; "Close run <no> is <status>" for a latest run that is Queued, Running, Blocked, Failed or Cancelled; "Close run out of date, run it again: <n> contracts" with the count; never waivable — a `WAIVED` or `NOT_APPLICABLE` item clears nothing and ERR-14 names the gate; the result a sandbox replay takes from the source lock carries `replayed`, and a source lock without the result leaves the sandbox's own evaluation (05 SBX-04 rev 1.111)

  - **Prerequisites:** CLO-13
  - **Scope:**

    - Schema: none for the acknowledgement and the retry (no column, type, grant, enum literal, transition pair or migration; the exception code `JOURNAL_EXPORT_FAILED` is a row of 04 table 15.4-B, PRD IMP-130; a refused batch reaches `failed` through `exported` inside one transaction, supervisor ruling R-71 (b)). Rev 1.20 (item JRN-FAILED-CANCEL-1): Alembic revision 0108 re-renders the DB-03 pairs of `journal_run` and `journal_batch` with E-34 `failed` → `cancelled`. Rev 1.34 (item JR-CLOSED-PERIOD-GUARD-1; rulings R-97 (7) and R-112 (b) (6)): Alembic revision 0104 adds two triggers, each over a new function — the DB-07 `tg_journal_run__period_guard` and the DB-16 `tg_journal_line__batch_draft`; no table, column, type or grant changes
    - API: API-R-38 `POST /journal-batches/{id}/acknowledge`, `POST /journal-batches/{id}/retry` (202); `POST /journal-runs/{id}/cancel` gains two refusals, each 409 `invalid-transition` (04 §16.7 rev 1.127; supervisor rulings R-52 (b), R-32 and R-71 (a)). Rev 1.20 (item JRN-FAILED-CANCEL-1; ruling R-112 (c); 04 rev 1.159): `cancel` answers 202 API-S-Job for a `failed` run (PRD ERR-73, ERR-74); `POST /journal-batches/{id}/hand-over` (202); `GET /journal-batches/{id}/download` recounts a batch it renders (PRD ERR-79). Rev 1.33 (item JRN-CANCEL-APPROVED-PENDING-1; ruling R-119 (c); 04 rev 1.204): `cancel` gains a third refusal for a run without a failed batch — a batch waits to be sent again after a failed attempt (04 §16.7 (c)). Rev 1.34 (item JR-CLOSED-PERIOD-GUARD-1; 04 rev 1.205): `POST /journal-runs` and `cancel` answer 409 `period-closed` for a period that is `closed` or `permanently_locked` (PRD ERR-86), and `POST /journal-runs` answers 422 on `period_key` for a `future` period (PRD SM-08's guard). Rev 1.41 (item JRN-RETRY-CLAIMED-1; 04 rev 1.221): `retry` answers 409 `invalid-transition` while the batch's export message is claimed and writes no second message while that message is still to be sent or due again; the `JOURNAL_EXPORT` job's `result` gains `waiting`, the batches that are sent again later with the instant; the `batches` members of API-S-JournalRun gain `handed_over_at`. Rev 1.52 (item JRN-RUN-RETRY-1; 04 rev 1.244; 05 rev 1.173): `POST /journal-runs/{id}/export` retries the failed batches of its run under the rule of `retry` — a retry message where a batch's message is settled, nothing where it is still to be sent or due again, and 409 `invalid-transition` for the whole command, nothing written, beside a claimed one. Rev 1.54 (item JRN-EXIT-SETTLE-1; 04 rev 1.247; 05 rev 1.175): `cancel` of a failed run and `hand-over` answer 409 `invalid-transition` for 15 minutes after a failed batch's last attempt ended without the ledger's refusal, and at any age for a batch the ledger accepted and does not show; the mock gains the ADP-21 kind `READ_LAG` and `POST /__mocks__/netsuite/__erp/journals/release`
    - Engine: none
    - Screens: none

      - `backend/tests/domain/journals/test_export.py::test_req_plt_034_journal_batch_exported_is_delivered` — an endpoint registered through `POST /webhook-endpoints` for the two journal batch kinds gets one `journal_batch.exported` delivery when a batch is exported, with `data` `{id, href, journal_run_id, journal_run_href}`; the worker posts the signed envelope and `GET /webhook-deliveries` shows it `SUCCEEDED`; `::test_req_plt_034_journal_batch_acknowledged_is_delivered` — recording the ERP reference writes one `journal_batch.acknowledged` delivery, a refused acknowledgement none; a refused batch emits neither kind (`::test_permanent_failure`) and an ERP receipt emits both (`::test_last_transient_attempt_fails_the_batch`) (REQ-PLT-034; supervisor ruling R-75 (c))

the job asks the ledger for the failed batch, finds none, and run and batch are `cancelled` with the exception item resolved; the period is calculated again as a `draft` with a new run number and new external ids, journal entry numbers continue, and after approval and export the ledger holds the period once; `::test_a_batch_the_ledger_holds_is_acknowledged_and_the_cancel_refused` — the ledger took the chunk although the batch is `failed`: `DUPLICATE` receipt, batch and run `acknowledged`, the job `SUCCEEDED_WITH_EXCEPTIONS` with PRD ERR-73 in `result.refusal`, nothing cancelled; `::test_an_unreachable_ledger_changes_nothing` — the attempt fails, the job is queued again, run and batch stay `failed`; `::test_a_run_partly_in_a_ledger_is_not_cancelled_and_its_failed_batch_is_handed_over` — one chunk acknowledged, one failed: the cancel answers 409 PRD ERR-74 and starts no job; the hand-over stores the batch's file, the batch is `exported` without a message or a receipt, `BATCHES_ACKNOWLEDGED` counts it, and `acknowledge` with the ledger's document ends batch and run `acknowledged`; `::test_a_csv_batch_is_not_handed_over_and_its_failed_run_is_cancelled_without_a_ledger`; `::test_a_cancel_waits_for_a_retry_and_is_not_asked_twice`; `::test_a_batch_that_gained_a_line_is_neither_handed_over_nor_downloaded` — the recount refuses the hand-over, and the download of the rendered batch answers 409 PRD ERR-79; rev 1.41, item JRN-RETRY-CLAIMED-1 (04 §16.7 `retry` rev 1.221; 05 ADP-32 rev 1.160): `backend/tests/domain/journals/test_failed_run_exit.py::test_a_batch_whose_message_is_claimed_is_not_retried` — the worker stops after the batch was recorded `failed` and before its message was recorded `DEAD`: the retry answers 409 by the batch's name and writes no second message, the hand-over and the cancel are refused beside the claim, and 15 minutes after the claim the message is dispatched again — it asks the ledger first, posts, and batch and run are `acknowledged` with one posting; `::test_a_message_due_again_after_a_stop_is_the_retry_and_gets_no_second_message` — the same stop one step on: the message is taken again, the ledger does not answer and the message is due again; the retry answers 202 and writes no second message, the exits are refused as beside any retry, and the message's next attempt posts; `::test_the_export_job_says_which_batches_wait_and_from_when` — of two chunks the ledger answers 500 for one: the job's `result.waiting` names it with the instant it is due, names it still when asked before that instant, and is empty once it is sent; a second retry beside the claim of a retry's own message is refused in `::test_a_batch_being_sent_is_neither_handed_over_nor_its_run_cancelled`; `backend/tests/unit/journals/test_failed_exits_rules.py::test_the_retry_row_states_its_refusals_word_for_word`; its job sends both and batch and run are `acknowledged`; a second press writes nothing more — where before the item the command wrote nothing and its job ended `SUCCEEDED` over a run that stayed `failed`; The world tells each mock the invoice the ERP posts itself under `billing.posting = ERP` — INV-UK-7001 at the 20 August spot, Dr 1100 / Cr 2100 GBP 45,360.00; the subledger states the stored closing contract liability, 0.00 (billed USD 54,000.00 less revenue USD 54,000.00); the ledger holds −540.00 (43,740.00 + 1,080.00 − 45,360.00); the row differs by −540.00 with one item `OTHER` and the variance is 1; accounts 4000 and 7200 tie, and the pull answers GBP alone and is not refused. The −540.00 is July's period-end remeasurement — the layer carried at the July closing rate, 44,280.00 against 43,740.00 — which only a close run of July posts, and this world runs journal runs and no close run (43,740.00 + 540.00 + 1,080.00 − 45,360.00 = 0.00). The witness above keeps every step but its last, whose expected value the supervisor ruled a STALE EXPECTATION (2026-10-02 00:09): the late invoice of January is stored and applied as a billing event, and BOTH kinds are out of date — under `billing.posting = ERP` the event writes no subledger line and, computed, moves the stored closing contract liability, so the reviewed subledger-to-GL comparison is of a balance the subledger no longer states. "Billing to subledger only" stays stated in 04 and pinned without a database: the import stores no document without applying it to its contract. The witnesses:
        - `backend/tests/domain/close/test_reconciliation_overtaken.py::test_a_posting_that_began_before_a_generation_and_committed_after_it_overtakes_it` — two cases: a unit of work begins at T and posts two lines of January, the subledger-to-GL reconciliation is generated, compared and reviewed at T + 1 s while it is open — its lines written after the generation, or written and uncommitted at it — and it commits afterwards. The lines carry `recorded_at` = T, earlier than the reconciliation's `as_of_known_at`; the posting was sealed after the position the generation read, and the gate names both reconciliations out of date; generated again it passes. Fail-first, both cases: the gate named billing to subledger alone and counted the subledger-to-GL reconciliation as reviewed, two lines short of the ledger
        - `backend/tests/domain/close/test_reconciliation_overtaken.py::test_an_attach_is_refused_over_a_posting_sealed_after_the_generation` — a posting begun before the generation of a `DRAFT` and committed after it: the attach is refused by name over its two lines, which the reconciliation does not hold, although they were recorded before its `as_of_known_at`
        - `backend/tests/domain/close/test_reconciliation_overtaken.py::test_a_billing_event_that_writes_no_line_overtakes_both_reconciliations` — WLD-K-01 under `billing.posting = ERP`: an invoice of January recorded after both reviews writes no subledger line, and the gate names both out of date — billing to subledger by its count of billing events, subledger to GL by its number of the entity's events through the period; generated again, the billing reconciliation shows a second `UNMATCHED_SUBLEDGER` and the contract liability row states −103,930.14 against a ledger at −103,430.14, and with the invoice in the ledger both are reviewed and the gate passes. February's invoice of the same contract leaves January's gate as it is. Fail-first: the gate read passed after the January invoice
        - `backend/tests/domain/close/test_reconciliation_overtaken.py::test_an_event_recorded_before_a_generation_and_computed_after_the_review_overtakes_it` — the supervisor's ruling of 2026-10-02 01:41: the January invoice is recorded and its group waits for a computation; both kinds are generated and reviewed while it waits — billing to subledger shows the invoice, subledger to GL ties on the balance of before — and the gate passes; the group is computed, no line is written, and the gate names "Subledger to GL" alone out of date, by the second term of its number (the event's inclusion in a contract version of the book); generated again the contract liability row states −103,930.14 against −103,430.14, and with the invoice in the ledger the gate passes. Fail-first, with the count of events alone: the gate read passed after the computation, the position and the count unchanged
        - `backend/tests/domain/close/test_reconciliation_overtaken.py::test_an_event_recorded_and_computed_while_a_generation_runs_is_not_counted_as_read` — on the record clock, as under the wall clock: the January invoice is recorded and computed while the ledger kind's generation runs, after its instant is fixed and before it counts; the attach ties on the balance of before; the gate names "Subledger to GL" out of date, because a generation counts the rows at or before its own instant and the gate every row; generated again the contract liability row states −103,930.14 against −103,430.14. Fail-first, with the generation's count unbounded: the gate read passed
        - `backend/tests/domain/close/test_reconciliation_overtaken.py::test_an_event_that_began_before_a_generation_and_committed_after_it_overtakes_it` — the mirror of that case (the supervisor's word of 2026-10-02 05:34), on the record clock: the January invoice is recorded and computed in a unit of work that begins before both generations and commits after both reviews; its record time lies before each reconciliation's instant, it writes no line and no posting is sealed; a generation does not count a row it does not read, the gate counts every row, and the gate names both kinds out of date; generated again the contract liability row states −103,930.14 against −103,430.14. Fail-first, on the item's own build: with the ledger kind's number not recorded the gate named billing to subledger alone, and with the billing kind's numbers not recorded subledger to GL alone
        - `backend/tests/unit/close/test_reconciliation_reads.py` — without a database, the statements as PostgreSQL receives them: the whole "out of date" condition; the generation's reads under the conditions the gate counts under, the ledger kind's two terms in one expression, bounded by the reconciliation's instant for a generation and unbounded for the gate; every row read is counted (a version, a void); the chain position is a plain read and 0 for a book that has sealed nothing; the subledger side by the position, and by the time for a row that holds none
        - `backend/tests/pg/test_close_tables.py::test_reconciliation_records_what_it_read` — revision 0121: the three columns are written by the INSERT, NULL in a row that names none, and not updatable
        - `backend/tests/domain/contracts/test_fx_journal_export.py::test_role_basis_a_foreign_currency_entity_ties_once_julys_close_has_run` (the module is CLO-15's) — the supervisor's ruling of 2026-10-02 01:41, a test of its own beside R-110's witness: AVM-UK's world with the one thing that witness leaves out, July's close run. The run `SUCCEEDED`; its FX pass posts the period-end remeasurement Dr 2100 / Cr 7200 GBP 540.00, and its reclassification presents the layer as a contract asset on 31 July and takes that back on 1 August; INV-UK-7001 settles the layer at spot; at 31 August the subledger's ledger holds 45,360.00 on account 2100 (43,740.00 + 540.00 + 1,080.00), which the invoice the ERP posts itself credits in full.       - rev 1.21, `backend/tests/domain/close/test_close_run_steps.py::test_release_schedules_posts_the_loss_provision_of_the_period_end` — the release step posts what only a period end decides (R-79 (a), (d)): WLD-K-03 with an estimate at completion of 1,300,000.00 has an expected loss of 100,000.00 against its unconstrained consideration of 1,200,000.00 and a loss through margin to date of 96,923.08 (costs 420,000.00 less revenue 323,076.92), so the required provision at 30 Sep 2026 is 3,076.92 (ENGINE_SPEC_B S11-R-14, S11-R-16); the step posts Dr `LOSS_EXPENSE` 3,076.92 / Cr `LOSS_PROVISION` 3,076.92 as the group's `CLOSE_RELEASE` posting `release:<close run>:<group>`, dated the period end, with no computation, contract version or stored trace; periods close in order (supervisor ruling R-112), so August's run comes first and posts the reclass of the contract asset 50,000.00 dated 31 Aug 2026 with its reversal on 1 Sep 2026 and no provision, and September's run posts the provision and nothing else
      - rev 1.21, `backend/tests/domain/close/test_close_run_steps.py::test_a_second_release_of_a_loss_unit_posts_nothing` — a second close run over an unchanged loss contract posts nothing (05 RCP-06; ENGINE_SPEC_B S14-INV-02): the database witness of the loss-unit family owed under supervisor rulings R-11 and R-44 (b); red until `subledger_line.subject_key` (Alembic revision 0082, lane ENG-FX) is on main

Until the ledger stored the subject key the engine's later passes emitted August's provision again with the reversal of the posted one, two entries of August that cancelled in every role, and the test showed that September's run was not refused over them. The passes now emit nothing for August: this world holds no net-zero set, and the test stays green when the rule counts any amount of an earlier period instead of the net (measured at the build with that mutation), so it is no witness of the rule. The rule of ruling R-114 (a) is held by the pure cases of `backend/tests/unit/close/test_period_end_order.py` — which earlier period refuses a step: net per account role and currency, transaction and functional, the earliest named — and of `backend/tests/unit/close/test_period_ends.py`; four of their nodes fail under the same mutation

      - `backend/tests/domain/close/test_close_run_steps.py::test_netting_reclass_auto_reversing` — for a contract with unbilled receivable 3,000.00 and contract asset 2,000.00 at period end, `NETTING_RECLASS` posts Dr unbilled receivable 3,000.00, Dr contract asset 2,000.00 / Cr contract liability 5,000.00 and its reversal in the next period (POLICIES CHK-010; JET-06; D-13); rev 1.21: one `NETTING_RECLASS` posting of the entity `reclass:<close run>:<entity>`, the reclass dated the last day of the period and the reversal the first day of the next; a second run posts nothing, and the next period's run posts that period's reclass and no second reversal

      - RC-SMOKE.4 Import findings: `maya` opens the WLD-B-04 import `avm-us-progress-2026-09-invalid.csv` on SF-10:detail, step `validate`. The heading "Rejected: fix the file and upload again" (`SF-10-banner-rejected`) is shown, and grid "Import rows" (`SF-10-grid-rows`) holds two `PROGRESS_OVER_DELIVERY` messages (Progress events, CSV v2 template `progress_events`, SCREENS §12.2; emitter DIN-9; seeded by DIN-15)
      - RC-SMOKE.5 Contract workbench: `maya` finds `SF-ORD-10001` in grid "Contracts" on SF-02 `/contracts` and opens it. SF-03 shows `SF-03-kpi-strip` with its six cells, each an Explain trigger, and the cell 6 secondary triggers Contract asset and Unbilled receivable, eight Explain triggers in all (SCREENS §4.1.4; OQ-S-02; D-89 L7-3-Q-30), and `SF-03-tracker`. Selecting O1 opens region "Obligation details: …" (`SF-03-pane-obligation`)
      - RC-SMOKE.6 Schedules: on K-01 SF-03:schedules, grid "Revenue schedule" holds the FY2026-P09 line of O1 at 9,764.38. Its Explain trigger opens the Explain panel showing 9,764.38, and Esc returns focus to the trigger (WLD-X-02). On K-02 `SF-ORD-10002`, the FY2026-P09 line is 9,863.01 (WLD-X-05). On SF-04 `/schedules?entity=AVM-US&period=FY2026-P09&book=ASC606&rows=obligation` (SCR-URL-24; SCREENS_B §4.1 wireframe Rows: Obligation; D-88), row `SF-04-row-sf-ord-10001-o1` shows Sep 2026 9,764.38, and the run data row `obligation:SF-ORD-10001:O1` carries "9764.38" in the FY2026-P09 period column (E2E-02)
      - RC-SMOKE.7 Journal run: on SF-06 `/journals`, `maya` opens "Run journals", keeps entity AVM-US, selects book "ASC 606", period "Sep 2026" and mode "Gross", and chooses "Calculate journals". The toast "Journals calculated for AVM-US Sep 2026." offers "Open run". SF-06:run shows chip Calculated, the Difference KPI (`SF-06-kpi-difference`) at 0.00 with chip Balanced, and every `SF-06-grid-balance-checks` row with difference 0.00. "Submit for approval" shows the toast "Submitted journal run <run no> for approval."
      - RC-SMOKE.8 Journal approval: `priya` approves the `JOURNAL_RUN` request on SF-12:request. `GET /journal-runs/{id}` gives `approved_at` set and state `approved` or `exported`, because the approval writes one JOURNAL_EXPORT outbox message per batch (REQ-JE-013; S14-R-22; 05 ADP-30; D-89 L7-3-Q-31). SF-06:run shows chip Approved or Exported (E-34)
      - RC-SMOKE.9 CSV export: the relay exports the approved run. `GET /journal-runs/{id}` reaches `exported`, and every batch is `exported` with adapter `CSV`. SF-06:run shows chip Exported and no primary export action (SCREENS_B §3.2), and KPI "Batches acknowledged" shows "0 of <m>", where m is the API batch count. On SF-06:run-batches, grid "Batches" lists every batch with adapter "CSV" and state Exported. "Download" on the first batch returns a ZIP holding one CSV and a JSON manifest: `row_count` equals the CSV data rows, the debit total equals the credit total, and `sha256` equals the SHA-256 of the CSV bytes (ADP-33). "More actions" › "Export again" with Target "CSV download" (`SF-06-export-target`) shows the toast "The export was already recorded. No batch was posted again.", and every batch keeps its `state`, `exported_at`, `detail_sha256`, `row_version` and acknowledgement count (CTL-021 path)
      - RC-SMOKE.10 Waterfall and RPO reports: on SF-08:report `/reports/revenue_waterfall`, `marcus` sets AVM-US, ASC 606 and Sep 2026 and chooses "Run report". The run stamp (`SF-08-run-stamp`) is present. Row `SF-08-row-sf-ord-10001` shows Sep 2026 9,764.38 (WLD-X-02). The tie-out strip (`SF-08-banner-tie-outs`) lists `TO_WATERFALL_EQ_JE_REVENUE` with expected, actual and difference (RV-05). The cell opens the Explain contributor list, and Esc returns focus to the cell (RV-09; SB-R-07). On `/reports/rpo` as of 30 Sep 2026, row `SF-08-row-sf-ord-10417` shows total 105,043.80 (K-09; PRD J-15.4), and its time-band values sum to that total

      - `backend/tests/unit/approvals/test_seeded_admission.py::test_a_seeded_rule_set_is_read_for_a_signed_in_member_only` and `::test_the_basis_of_a_bootstrap_approval_is_stated_for_a_member_only` — no job, API client or operator reaches a seeded rule set, and the basis of a bootstrap approval is stated for a member only, so the rule approves no request without a preparer
